spec.forProvider.inputAttachments[].inputSettings.audioSelector[].selectorSettings.audioLanguageSelection.languageSelectionPolicy <string>	# How to handle a change in the language of the audio stream after it is selected. When set to strict, the transport stream demux strictly identifies audio streams by their language descriptor. If a PMT update occurs such that an audio stream matching the initially selected language is no longer present, then mute is encoded until the language returns. When set to loose, the demux chooses another audio stream in the program with the same stream type on a PMT update if it cannot find one with the same language.

spec.initProvider <object>	# THIS IS A BETA FIELD. It will be honored unless the Management Policies feature flag is disabled. InitProvider holds the same fields as ForProvider, with the exception of Identifier and other resource reference fields. The fields that are in InitProvider are merged into ForProvider when the resource is created. The same fields are also added to the terraform ignore_changes hook, to avoid updating them after creation. This is useful for fields that are required on creation, but we do not desire to update them after creation, for example because of an external controller is managing them, like an autoscaler.

For example, 0.1 means 10% white (90% black). For the input to be considered black, every pixel in a frame must be below this threshold. The range is 0.0 to 1.0, with any number of decimal places.

status.atProvider.inputAttachments[].inputSettings.audioSelector[].selectorSettings.audioLanguageSelection.languageSelectionPolicy <string>	# How to handle a change in the language of the audio stream after it is selected. When set to strict, the transport stream demux strictly identifies audio streams by their language descriptor. If a PMT update occurs such that an audio stream matching the initially selected language is no longer present, then mute is encoded until the language returns. When set to loose, the demux chooses another audio stream in the program with the same stream type on a PMT update if it cannot find one with the same language.
